List of awesome open source applications for macOS. This list contains a lot of native, and cross-platform apps. The main goal of this repository is to find free open source apps and start contributing. Feel free to [contribute](CONTRIBUTING.md) to the list, any suggestions are welcome!

To receive all new or popular applications you can join our [telegram channel](https://t.me/opensourcemacosapps).

* [Show Desktop](https://github.com/stanczykwyatt/show-desktop) ⭐ 7 | 🐛 9 | 🌐 Swift | 📅 2026-11-03 - Hides every window with one click or a shortcut to show the Desktop, then brings them all back exactly as they were. ([Website](https://stanczykwyatt.github.io/show-desktop/)) — `Swift`
* [DockFlip](https://github.com/geezery/DockFlip) ⭐ 6 | 🐛 0 | 🌐 Swift | 📅 2026-11-12 - Dock window lists with browser tabs, a Flip 3D ⌘Tab window switcher and Windows-style snapping in one app. ([Website](https://geezery.github.io/DockFlip/)) — `Swift`
* [mFix](https://github.com/Zingzy/mFix) ⭐ 6 | 🐛 0 | 🌐 Swift | 📅 2026-10-20 - Window snapping with layouts, a Cmd+Tab window switcher with live previews, Dock window previews and Cut and paste in Finder. — `Swift`
* [SimpleScreenTab](https://github.com/k33ssa/SimpleScreenTab) ⭐ 5 | 🐛 0 | 🌐 Swift | 📅 2026-11-14 - Per-monitor Cmd-Tab app switcher that shows only the apps on the display under the mouse. — `Swift`
* [WindowCycle](https://github.com/WhiteMinds/window-cycle) ⭐ 5 | 🐛 0 | 🌐 Swift | 📅 2026-06-20 - A focused macOS current-app window switcher with a visible panel. ([Website](https://github.com/WhiteMinds/window-cycle) ⭐ 5 | 🐛 0 | 🌐 Swift | 📅 2026-06-20) — `Swift`
